# TECHDIAG-LIST.txt - known problems, update numbers and codes, for TechDiag
# Made By Tzahi Bocal
# Its place: <home>\TechDiag\TECHDIAG-LIST.txt (the stick's root, C:\tech or the network folder is
# the home). TechDiag has the same list built in and works without this file; it uses this file only
# when its DATE is the same as, or newer than, the built-in one. It is data only - nothing in it is run.
# It can be changed in Notepad. A line that starts with # is a note. Each other line is one item: its
# type, then its fields, with | between them. Use plain letters, digits, spaces and
# . , - _ / ( ) : + '  only. A line that is not right is not used, and the report says how many
# lines were not used.
#
#   DATE  | the date of this list (DD/MM/YYYY) - shown in the report
#   BUILD | the build of Windows (or of .NET) | its update
#   KB    | the update | PROBLEM or CHECK | what happens | what to do
#           Give the update of a KB line (and the update its action names)
#           its BUILD lines too: then, once a newer update is installed, the
#           old one is shown as replaced and not as a problem.
#           In Recovery, for an update that waits: an action that starts
#           with In Recovery: (or names FIXCMD) takes the place of FIXCMD; any
#           other action is shown as a step in Windows.
#   APP   | the name of the app | its version | PROBLEM or CHECK | what happens | what to do
#   CODE  | the code (0x and 8 digits) | what it means, in a few words
#
DATE  | 18/11/2026
#

# ---- Blue screen codes
CODE  | 0x0000009F | a driver or power problem
CODE  | 0x0000007E | a driver crashed
CODE  | 0x0000001E | a driver crashed
CODE  | 0x0000003B | a driver or system file crashed
CODE  | 0x000000D1 | a driver used memory it should not
CODE  | 0x0000000A | a driver or memory problem
CODE  | 0x00000050 | a memory or driver problem
CODE  | 0x0000001A | a memory problem - test the memory
CODE  | 0x00000124 | a hardware error - CPU, memory or BIOS
CODE  | 0x0000009C | a hardware error - CPU or memory
CODE  | 0x00000101 | a CPU or BIOS problem
CODE  | 0x00000133 | a driver or the disk did not answer in time
CODE  | 0x000000EF | a part of Windows stopped - check the disk
CODE  | 0x000000F4 | a part of Windows stopped - check the disk
CODE  | 0x0000007B | Windows cannot reach the boot disk
CODE  | 0x000000C2 | a driver problem
CODE  | 0x00000139 | a driver or memory problem
CODE  | 0x00000116 | the display driver stopped
CODE  | 0x000000ED | Windows cannot open the boot disk - check the disk
CODE  | 0xC000021A | a main part of Windows stopped - a damaged system file or update
CODE  | 0x0000007A | data could not be read from the disk - check the disk
CODE  | 0x00000077 | data could not be read from the disk - check the disk
CODE  | 0x00000024 | a file system problem - check the disk
CODE  | 0x00000154 | a disk or memory problem - check the disk
CODE  | 0x0000012B | a memory hardware error - test the memory
CODE  | 0x00000019 | a driver or memory problem
CODE  | 0x0000004E | a memory or driver problem
CODE  | 0x000000FE | a USB driver or device problem
CODE  | 0x00000144 | a USB driver or device problem
CODE  | 0x00000119 | the display driver or the graphics card
CODE  | 0x000000EA | the display driver or the graphics card
CODE  | 0x0000014F | a part of Windows or a driver did not answer at wake from sleep - a driver or BIOS problem
#
# ---- Update codes
CODE  | 0x800F0922 | not enough room on the EFI partition, or no connection to the update server
CODE  | 0x800F081F | files the update needs are missing
CODE  | 0x800F0831 | a part of the update store is missing
CODE  | 0x800F0983 | a part of the update store is missing
CODE  | 0x800F0986 | the update could not be applied
CODE  | 0x800F0988 | the update store needs a cleanup
CODE  | 0x80073712 | the update store is damaged
CODE  | 0x80073701 | a part of the update store is missing
CODE  | 0x8007000D | an update file or the update store is damaged
CODE  | 0x80070002 | a file was not found
CODE  | 0x80070003 | a folder was not found
CODE  | 0x80070005 | access was denied
CODE  | 0x8007000E | not enough memory
CODE  | 0x80070020 | a file was in use by another program
CODE  | 0x80070070 | not enough free space on the disk
CODE  | 0x80070422 | a service the update needs is turned off
CODE  | 0x80070643 | the installer failed
CODE  | 0x80070652 | another install was running
CODE  | 0x800705B4 | it took too long
CODE  | 0x80240017 | the update does not apply to this PC
CODE  | 0x80240022 | all the updates of this run failed
CODE  | 0x8024402C | the update server could not be reached
CODE  | 0x80244022 | the update server was busy
CODE  | 0x80244019 | the update was not found on the update server
CODE  | 0x8024401C | the update server did not answer in time
CODE  | 0x80240438 | the update server could not be reached
CODE  | 0x80072EE2 | the connection to the update server timed out
CODE  | 0x80072EFD | the update server could not be reached
CODE  | 0x80072EFE | the connection to the update server was cut
CODE  | 0x80242016 | the update did not finish after the restart
CODE  | 0x80246007 | the update was not downloaded
CODE  | 0x8024200D | the download was not complete

# ---- table stop-codes | ms-bugcheck | 15/07/2025
# More stop codes, by their official names (added by the knowledge pack).
# The codes and their names: Microsoft, "Bug check code reference" (learn.microsoft.com; source in the
# public repository MicrosoftDocs/windows-driver-docs, licensed CC BY 4.0), page dated 15/07/2025, read 12/11/2026.
# The words after a name are TechDiag's own: the class of cause the name points to, not the cause.

# ---- table param | pack | 13/11/2026
# What a parameter of a stop code is, where Microsoft documents it (added by the knowledge pack): a
# Windows status code (its words are a CODE line above), or a value the page lists (TechDiag's own words).
# The source, read first-hand page by page: Microsoft Learn, "Bug check code reference" and the page of each stop
# code (the public repository MicrosoftDocs/windows-driver-docs, windows-driver-docs-pr/debugger, licensed CC BY
# 4.0), read 13/11/2026. The date after each code is the page's own date (ms.date).
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x31 | the code of a program file in memory was found damaged (image relocation fix-up table)
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x3F | data read back into memory from the disk failed its check (CRC error)
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x403 | the memory tables of Windows were found out of step (page table and page frame numbers)
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x41790 | a page table of memory was found damaged
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x41792 | a damaged page table entry of memory was found
PARAM | 0x0000001A | 1 | 0x61941 | the paging tables of memory were found damaged
PARAM | 0x0000001E | 1 | STATUS | the error that was not handled (exception code)
PARAM | 0x0000003B | 1 | STATUS | the error in a call to Windows (exception code)
PARAM | 0x0000007A | 2 | STATUS | the result of the read from the disk (I/O status)
PARAM | 0x0000007E | 1 | STATUS | the error that was not handled (exception code)
PARAM | 0x1000007E | 1 | STATUS | the error that was not handled (exception code)
PARAM | 0x0000008E | 1 | STATUS | the error that was not handled (exception code)
PARAM | 0x1000008E | 1 | STATUS | the error that was not handled (exception code)
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x1 | a device that was being removed still had a power request open
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x2 | a device finished a power request of the system without passing on the next one
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x3 | a device held a power request for too long (blocked power request)
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x4 | a power change waited too long for Plug and Play (the part of Windows that manages devices)
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x5 | a device did not finish a power change in time
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x6 | a device did not finish its power change successfully
PARAM | 0x0000009F | 1 | 0x500 | a device finished a power request of the system without passing on the next one
PARAM | 0x000000ED | 2 | STATUS | the result of opening the Windows volume (file system status)
PARAM | 0x00000116 | 3 | STATUS | the error of the last failed step of the display driver

PARAM | 0x00000124 | 1 | 0x1 | a machine check the hardware corrected (corrected machine check)
PARAM | 0x00000124 | 1 | 0x2 | a platform error the hardware corrected (corrected platform error)
PARAM | 0x00000124 | 1 | 0x3 | an urgent hardware signal (non-maskable interrupt, NMI)
